- **mod-reverse-engineering**: Read how a game actually works so a mod can hook it. Use when a mod needs game internals, e.g. "how does the boss AI work", "find the damage function", "what format is this .sld/.pak/.dat", "where is the player position in memory". ([trilex07/SingularityVR](https://github.com/trilex07/SingularityVR/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mod-reverse-engineering) · ★3)
